type CodecAacAdtsError int32
const (
	CodecAacAdtsErrorTruncated    CodecAacAdtsError = 0
	CodecAacAdtsErrorSyncword     CodecAacAdtsError = 1
	CodecAacAdtsErrorSampleRate   CodecAacAdtsError = 2
	CodecAacAdtsErrorFrameLength  CodecAacAdtsError = 3
	CodecAacAdtsErrorPacketLength CodecAacAdtsError = 4
)
func (self CodecAacAdtsError) Equal(other CodecAacAdtsError) bool
func (self CodecAacAdtsError) Error() string
func (self CodecAacAdtsError) GoString() string
func (self CodecAacAdtsError) String() string
